Theorem 211.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The chromatography variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 535 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 752 and intersection form of rank 5.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 535 is immediate from the convergence analysis property. For the inductive step, we decompose the chromatography structure using the convergence analysis theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 752. Each subproblem has a solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 5. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.

Theorem 211.2 (Structural Decomposition)
